	makeQuickSlider = function(parent, arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 54)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		local textLabel, frame2, flag4, fn26, scrollingFrame
		frame.Parent = parent
		textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Text = arg .. ": " .. tostring(arg4) .. (arg6 or "")
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
		textLabel.Font = UIFont.Body
		textLabel.TextSize = 12
		textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel.Parent = frame
		local frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame3.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 8)
		frame3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 32)
		frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderBg or Color3.fromRGB(46, 52, 63)
		frame3.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
		frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame3.Parent = frame
		corner(frame3, 6)
		local frame4 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame4.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((arg4 - arg2) / (arg3 - arg2), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
		frame4.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderFill or Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
		frame4.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		frame4.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame4.Parent = frame3
		corner(frame4, 6)
		frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
		frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		frame2.Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp((arg4 - arg2) / (arg3 - arg2), 0, 1), 0, 0.5, 0)
		frame2.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
		frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame2.ZIndex = 2
		frame2.Parent = frame3
		corner(frame2, 20)
		flag4 = false

		fn26 = function(arg7)
			local n10 = math.clamp((arg7 - frame3.AbsolutePosition.X) / frame3.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
			local n11 = math.floor((arg2 + (arg3 - arg2) * n10) * 10 + 0.5) / 10
			frame4.Size = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 1, 0)
			frame2.Position = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 0.5, 0)
			textLabel.Text = arg .. ": " .. tostring(n11) .. (arg6 or "")

			if arg5 then
				arg5(n11)
			end
		end

		scrollingFrame = frame3:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")

		frame3.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				flag4 = true

				if scrollingFrame then
					scrollingFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
				end

				fn26(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)

		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				flag4 = false

				if scrollingFrame then
					scrollingFrame.ScrollingEnabled = true
				end
			end
		end)

		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if flag4 and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				fn26(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)

		local function fn27(arg7, arg8)
			arg7 = math.clamp(arg7, arg2, arg3)
			local n10 = (arg7 - arg2) / (arg3 - arg2)
			local n11 = math.floor(arg7 * 10 + 0.5) / 10
			frame4.Size = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 1, 0)
			frame2.Position = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 0.5, 0)
			textLabel.Text = arg .. ": " .. tostring(n11) .. (arg6 or "")

			if arg5 and not arg8 then
				arg5(n11)
			end
		end

		return { Set = fn27 }
	end

	function makeMainSliderWithInput(parent, text, arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 54)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame.Parent = parent
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		local frame2, frame3, flag4, fn26, v11
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.68, 0, 0, 18)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Text = text
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
		textLabel.Font = UIFont.Body
		textLabel.TextSize = 12
		textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel.Parent = frame
		local textBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
		textBox.Size = UDim2.new(0.28, 0, 0, 18)
		textBox.Position = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 0)
		textBox.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg
		textBox.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Input
		textBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
		textBox.Text = tostring(arg3) .. (arg5 or "")
		textBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
		textBox.Font = UIFont.Button
		textBox.TextSize = 10
		textBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		textBox.Parent = frame
		corner(textBox, 5)
		stroke(textBox, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.55)
		frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 8)
		frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 32)
		frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderBg or Color3.fromRGB(46, 52, 63)
		frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
		frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame2.Parent = frame
		corner(frame2, 6)
		local frame4 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame4.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((arg3 - arg) / (arg2 - arg), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
		frame4.BackgroundColor3 = arg6 or Theme.SliderFill or Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
		frame4.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		frame4.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame4.Parent = frame2
		corner(frame4, 6)
		frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame3.Name = "WhiteSliderKnob"
		frame3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
		frame3.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		frame3.Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp((arg3 - arg) / (arg2 - arg), 0, 1), 0, 0.5, 0)
		frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame3.ZIndex = 2
		frame3.Parent = frame2
		corner(frame3, 20)
		flag4 = false

		fn26 = function(arg7, arg8)
			local n10
			arg7 = math.clamp(arg7, arg, arg2)
			arg7 = math.floor(arg7 * 100 + 0.5) / 100
			n10 = (arg7 - arg) / (arg2 - arg)
			frame4.Size = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 1, 0)
			frame3.Position = UDim2.new(n10, 0, 0.5, 0)

			if not arg8 then
				textBox.Text = tostring(arg7) .. (arg5 or "")
			end

			if arg4 then
				arg4(arg7)
			end
		end

		v11 = frame2.FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA(frame2, "ScrollingFrame")

		frame2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				flag4 = true

				if v11 then
					v11.ScrollingEnabled = false
				end

				local n10 = math.clamp((input.Position.X - frame2.AbsolutePosition.X) / frame2.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
				fn26(arg + (arg2 - arg) * n10)
			end
		end)

		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				flag4 = false

				if v11 then
					v11.ScrollingEnabled = true
				end
			end
		end)

		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if flag4 and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				local n10 = math.clamp((input.Position.X - frame2.AbsolutePosition.X) / frame2.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
				fn26(arg + (arg2 - arg) * n10)
			end
		end)

		textBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
			local str = textBox.Text:gsub("[^%d%.]", "")
			local num = tonumber(str)

			if num then
				fn26(num)
			else
				textBox.Text = tostring(arg3) .. (arg5 or "")
			end
		end)

		return frame
	end

	function makeMainToggle(parent, text, arg, arg2, arg3)
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
		local textLabel, textButton, v11, textLabel2, v12, fn26
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame.Parent = parent
		textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Text = text
		textLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
		textLabel.Font = UIFont.Label
		textLabel.TextSize = 12
		textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		textLabel.Parent = frame
		textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
		textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
		textButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, -11)
		textButton.BackgroundTransparency = arg and 0 or 0.35
		textButton.BackgroundColor3 = arg and (arg3 or Theme.ToggleOn or Theme.Accent) or Theme.ToggleOff
		textButton.Text = ""
		textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
		textButton.Parent = frame
		corner(textButton, 6)
		stroke(textButton, arg and (arg3 or Theme.ToggleOn or Theme.Accent) or Theme.Stroke, 1, 0)
		v11 = textButton.FindFirstChildOfClass(textButton, "UIStroke")
		textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		textLabel2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -1)
		textLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel2.Text = "✓"
		textLabel2.TextColor3 = Theme.ToggleKnobOn or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		textLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel2.TextSize = 12
		textLabel2.Visible = arg and true or false
		textLabel2.Parent = textButton
		local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")
		textButton2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38)
		textButton2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0.5, -19)
		textButton2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textButton2.Text = ""
		textButton2.Parent = frame
		v12 = arg

		fn26 = function(arg4, arg5)
			v12 = arg4

			tw(textButton, {
				BackgroundColor3 = v12 and (arg3 or Theme.ToggleOn or Theme.Accent) or Theme.ToggleOff,
				BackgroundTransparency = v12 and 0 or 0.35,
			}, 0.1)

			textLabel2.Visible = v12 and true or false

			if v11 then
				v11.Color = v12 and (arg3 or Theme.ToggleOn or Theme.Accent) or Theme.Stroke
			end

			if arg5 ~= false and arg2 then
				arg2(v12)
			end
		end

		textButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			fn26(not v12, true)
		end)

		textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			fn26(not v12, true)
		end)

		BoundToggles[text] = function(arg4, arg5)
			if typeof(arg4) == "boolean" then
				fn26(arg4, arg5)
			else
				fn26(not v12, true)
			end
		end

		return BoundToggles[text]
	end

	makeKeybindRow = function(parent, arg, text)
		text = text or arg
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 36)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		textLabel.TextSize = 12

		textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel.Parent = frame
		textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
		textButton.Name = "WhiteTextBtn"
		textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 20)
		textButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0.5, -10)
		textButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Red
		textButton.Text = "X"
		textButton.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textButton.TextSize = 10
		textButton.Parent = frame
		corner(textButton, 5)
		textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")
		textButton2.Name = "WhiteTextBtn"
		textButton2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 20)
		textButton2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -10)
		textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row
		textButton2.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Input
		textButton2.Text = Keybinds[arg] or "NONE"
		textButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		textButton2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textButton2.TextSize = 9
		textButton2.Parent = frame
		corner(textButton2, 5)
		stroke(textButton2, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.45)

		textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Keybinds[arg] = "NONE"
			mainConfig.keybinds[arg] = "NONE"
			saveConfig()
			textButton2.Text = "NONE"

			if updateMovementPanelLabels then
				updateMovementPanelLabels()
			end
		end)

		textButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			textButton2.Text = "..."
			_G._bindsRecording = true
			local connection_

			connection_ = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
				if gameProcessed then
					return
				end

				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					Keybinds[arg] = input.KeyCode.Name
					mainConfig.keybinds[arg] = input.KeyCode.Name
					saveConfig()
					textButton2.Text = input.KeyCode.Name

					if arg == "Open Menu" then
						UI.OpenMenuKey = input.KeyCode
					end

					connection_:Disconnect()

					task.defer(function()
						_G._bindsRecording = false
					end)

					if updateMovementPanelLabels then
						updateMovementPanelLabels()
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end

		findBestAnimalModel = function(arg)
			if not arg then
				return nil
			end
			local v11 = nil

			pcall(function()
				local animalData, plotsFolder, v12
				animalData = arg.animalData or arg
				plotsFolder = _G.PlotsFolder or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
				v12 = plotsFolder and plotsFolder.FindFirstChild(plotsFolder, animalData.plot)
				if not v12 then
					return
				end
				local v13 = v12.FindFirstChild(v12, "AnimalPodiums")
				local v14 = v13 and v13:FindFirstChild(animalData.slot)
				local base = v14 and v14:FindFirstChild("Base")
				if not base then
					return
				end
				local spawn = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn") or base:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or base
				local position = spawn.Position

				for i, child in ipairs(spawn:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Model") then
						local humanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

						if humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude < 6 then
							local animationController = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController") or child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							local n10 = 0

							for i2, descendant in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") and descendant.Transparency < 1 then
									n10 = n10 + 1
								end
							end

							if n10 > 0 or animationController then
								v11 = child
								return
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local n10, v15 = -1, nil

				for i, descendant in ipairs(v12.GetDescendants(v12)) do
					if not descendant:IsA("Model") or descendant.Name == "Base" then
						continue
					end

					if descendant:FindFirstChild("Base") or descendant:FindFirstChild("Spawn") then
						continue
					end
					local humanoidRootPart2 = descendant:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or descendant.PrimaryPart or descendant:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
					if not humanoidRootPart2 then
						continue
					end
					local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude
					if magnitude > 12 then
						continue
					end
					local n11 = -magnitude
					local animalIndex = animalData.animalIndex or animalData.index or ""

					if animalIndex ~= "" and (descendant.Name == animalIndex or descendant.Name:find(animalIndex, 1, true)) then
						n11 = n11 + 500
					end

					if (arg.petName or arg.name or "") ~= "" and descendant.Name:find(arg.petName or arg.name or "", 1, true) then
						n11 = n11 + 200
					end

					local animationController = descendant:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController") or descendant:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

					if animationController then
						n11 = n11 + 150
					end

					local n12 = 0

					for i2, descendant2 in ipairs(descendant:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant2:IsA("MeshPart") and descendant2.Transparency < 1 then
							n12 = n12 + 1
						end
					end

					if n12 > 0 then
						n11 = n11 + math.min(n12 * 10, 100)
					else
						if not animationController then
							n11 = n11 - 300
						end
					end

					if humanoidRootPart2:IsA("BasePart") and humanoidRootPart2.Transparency >= 1 and n12 == 0 then
						n11 = n11 - 300
					end

					if n11 > n10 then
						n10 = n11
						v15 = descendant
					end
				end

				v11 = v15
			end)

			return v11
		end

			fn28 = function()
				local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					return nil
				end
				local v11, huge, name = nil, math.huge, nil
				local v12, huge2 = nil, math.huge

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= player and player_.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart3 = player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoidRootPart3 then
							local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart3.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude < 150 then
								if magnitude < huge2 then
									huge2, v12 = magnitude, player_
								end

								local tool = player_.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
								local v13 = tool and fn26(tool.Name)

								if v13 and magnitude < huge then
									huge, v11, name = magnitude, player_, tool.Name
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if v11 then
					return v11, name, math.floor(huge)
				end

				if v12 then
					return v12, nil, math.floor(huge2)
				end
				return nil
			end

			fn29 = function()
				local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					return nil
				end
				local v11, huge, name = nil, math.huge, nil
				local v12, huge2, name2 = nil, math.huge, nil

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= player and player_.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart3 = player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local tool = player_.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

						if humanoidRootPart3 and tool and fn26(tool.Name) then
							local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart3.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude <= 20 then
								local str = tool.Name:lower()

								if str:find("slap", 1, true) or str:find("rainbowrath", 1, true) or str:find("rage", 1, true) then
									if magnitude < huge2 then
										huge2, v12, name2 = magnitude, player_, tool.Name
									end
								end

								if magnitude < huge then
									huge, v11, name = magnitude, player_, tool.Name
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if v12 then
					return v12, name2, math.floor(huge2)
				end

				if v11 then
					return v11, name, math.floor(huge)
				end
				return nil
			end

			tbl20 = nil
			tbl21 = nil
			tbl22 = nil

			local function fn32(arg)
				arg = tonumber(arg)
				if not arg or arg <= 0 then
					return nil
				end

				if arg >= 1000000000 then
					return string.format("$%.2fB/s", arg / 1000000000)
				end

				if arg >= 1000000 then
					return string.format("$%.2fM/s", arg / 1000000)
				end

				if arg >= 1000 then
					return string.format("$%.1fk/s", arg / 1000)
				end
				return "$" .. math.floor(arg) .. "/s"
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					if _G.BJK_HubGen ~= bjkHubGen then
						break
					end
					task.wait(0.5)
					if not frame.Visible then
						continue
					end

					pcall(function()
						local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not humanoidRootPart2 then
							return
						end

						for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
							if player_ ~= player and player_.Character then
								local tool = player_.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

								if tool then
									local str = tool.Name:lower()
									local humanoidRootPart3 = player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local magnitude = humanoidRootPart3 and (humanoidRootPart3.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

									if magnitude and magnitude <= 100 then
										if str:find("boogie", 1, true) and magnitude <= 80 then
											tbl21 = { plr = player_, dist = math.floor(magnitude), t = os.clock() }
										elseif str:find("airstrike", 1, true) or str:find("air strike", 1, true) then
											tbl22 = { plr = player_, dist = math.floor(magnitude), t = os.clock() }
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)

					if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
						local name, mutation, str = nil, nil, nil

						pcall(function()
							if _G.BJK_StealStatus and _G.BJK_StealStatus.target and _G.BJK_StealStatus.target.name then
								name = _G.BJK_StealStatus.target.name
								str = fn32(_G.BJK_StealStatus.target.mps or _G.BJK_StealStatus.target.value or _G.BJK_StealStatus.target.mpsValue)
							end
						end)

						pcall(function()
							if SharedState.SelectedPetData and (SharedState.SelectedPetData.petName or SharedState.SelectedPetData.name) then
								if not name then
									name = SharedState.SelectedPetData.petName or SharedState.SelectedPetData.name
								end

								if not mutation then
									mutation = SharedState.SelectedPetData.mutation
								end

								if not str then
									if SharedState.SelectedPetData.mpsText and tostring(SharedState.SelectedPetData.mpsText) ~= "" then
										local str2 = tostring(SharedState.SelectedPetData.mpsText):gsub("^%$", "")

										if not str2:lower():find("/s") then
											str2 = str2 .. "/s"
										end

										str = "$" .. str2
									else
										str = fn32(SharedState.SelectedPetData.mpsValue)
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						if not name then
							pcall(function()
								if _BJKAnimalNames and player.Character then
									for i, descendant in ipairs(player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
										if descendant:IsA("Model") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
											local v11 = _BJKAnimalNames[descendant.Name:lower()]
											if v11 then
												name = v11
												break
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end)
						end

						if name then
							tbl20 = {
								txt = tostring(name) .. (mutation and mutation ~= "" and tostring(mutation):lower() ~= "normal" and " " .. tostring(mutation) or ""),
								gen = str,
								t = os.clock(),
							}
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			local function fn33(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
				local now = os.clock()
				if now - (_G.__BJKSelfCmdT or -100) < 3 then
					return
				end

				if tbl17[arg] and now - tbl17[arg] < n10 then
					return
				end
				tbl17[arg] = now
				local v11, v12, v13

				if arg2 then
					v11, v12, v13 = arg2, arg3, arg4
				else
					v11, v12, v13 = fn28()
				end

				local v14 = arg

				if arg == "Ragdoll / Slap" and v12 then
					local v15 = fn26(v12)

					if v15 then
						v14 = v15
					end
				end

				local txt, gen = nil, nil

				if tbl20 and now - tbl20.t < 10 then
					txt = tbl20.txt
					gen = tbl20.gen
				end

				if not txt and v11 then
					local v15

					pcall(function()
						local v16, v17 = getStealingInfo(v11)

						if v17 and v17 ~= "Brainrot" then
							v15 = v17
						end
					end)

					if not v15 then
						pcall(function()
							if _BJKAnimalNames and v11.Character then
								for i, descendant in ipairs(v11.Character:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("Model") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
										local v16 = _BJKAnimalNames[descendant.Name:lower()]
										if v16 then
											v15 = v16
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					if v15 then
						local mutation, genText
						mutation, genText = nil, nil

						pcall(function()
							for i, v16 in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache or {}) do
								if v16 and v16.name and v16.name:lower() == tostring(v15):lower() then
									mutation = v16.mutation
									genText = v16.genText
									break
								end
							end
						end)

						txt = tostring(v15) .. (mutation and mutation ~= "" and tostring(mutation):lower() ~= "normal" and " " .. tostring(mutation) or "")

						if genText and tostring(genText) ~= "" then
							genText = tostring(genText):gsub("^%$", "")

							if not genText.lower(genText):find("/s") then
								genText = genText .. "/s"
							end

							gen = "$" .. genText
						end
					end
				end

				table.insert(tbl15, 1, {
					who = v11 and v11.DisplayName .. " (@" .. v11.Name .. ")" or "Unknown",
					userId = v11 and v11.UserId or nil,
					grief = v14,
					tool = v12,
					dist = v13,
					carried = txt,
					carriedGen = gen,
					t = now,
				})

				while #tbl15 > 20 do
					table.remove(tbl15)
				end

				fn31()

				pcall(function()
					local v15 = frame.FindFirstChildOfClass(frame, "UIStroke")

					if v15 then
						v15.Color = Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
						v15.Transparency = 0

						task.delay(1, function()
							v15.Color = Theme.Stroke
							v15.Transparency = 0.35
						end)
					end
				end)
			end

			local function fn34(arg, arg2)
				local attribute = arg:GetAttribute(arg2)
				if attribute == false or attribute == nil then
					return
				end
				local str = tostring(arg2):lower()
				if str == "stealing" or str == "stealingindex" then
					return
				end

				if str:sub(1, 7) == "ragdoll" then
					if type(attribute) == "number" and attribute <= Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
						return
					end
					local v11, v12, v13 = fn29()

					if v11 then
						fn33("Ragdoll / Slap", v11, v12, v13)
					elseif tbl22 and os.clock() - tbl22.t < 8 then
						fn33("Airstrike", tbl22.plr, "Airstrike", tbl22.dist)
					else
						fn33("Ragdoll / Slap")
					end

					return
				end

				for k in pairs(tbl18) do
					if k ~= "ragdoll" and str:sub(1, #k) == k then
						fn33(k:sub(1, 1):upper() .. k:sub(2) .. " (Admin Panel)")
						return
					end
				end

				local v11 = fn27(str)

				if v11 then
					fn33(v11)
				end
			end

			local function fn35(arg)
				if not arg then
					return
				end

				pcall(function()
					arg.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attribute)
						fn34(arg, attribute)
					end)
				end)
			end

			local function fn36(arg)
				if not arg then
					return
				end
				fn35(arg)

				pcall(function()
					arg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
						if descendant:IsA("Tool") or descendant:IsA("Accessory") then
							return
						end

						if descendant:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") then
							return
						end
						local str = descendant.Name:lower()

						for i, v11 in ipairs({ "balloon", "rocket", "jail", "jumpscare" }) do
							if str:find(v11, 1, true) then
								fn33(v11:sub(1, 1):upper() .. v11:sub(2) .. " (Admin Panel)")
								return
							end
						end

						local v11 = fn26(descendant.Name) or fn27(descendant.Name)

						if v11 then
							fn33(v11)
						end
					end)
				end)

				local humanoid2 = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid2 then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid2.StateChanged:Connect(function(old, new)
							if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
								local v11, v12, v13 = fn29()

								if v11 then
									fn33("Ragdoll / Slap", v11, v12, v13)
								elseif tbl22 and os.clock() - tbl22.t < 8 then
									fn33("Airstrike", tbl22.plr, "Airstrike", tbl22.dist)
								end
							end
						end)
					end)

					pcall(function()
						humanoid2.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg2)
							local str = ""

							pcall(function()
								str = (arg2.Animation and arg2.Animation.Name or "") .. " " .. (arg2.Name or "")
							end)

							str = str:lower()

							if str:find("boogie", 1, true) then
								fn33("Boogie Bomb")
							elseif str:find("dance", 1, true) or str:find("twerk", 1, true) then
								local v11, v12, v13 = fn28()

								if v11 and v12 and v13 and v13 <= 80 then
									fn33("Boogie Bomb")
								end
							end
						end)
					end)
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					local humanoidRootPart2 = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
					if not humanoidRootPart2 then
						return
					end
					local position = humanoidRootPart2.Position

					while humanoidRootPart2.Parent and arg.Parent do
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						local position2 = humanoidRootPart2.Position
						local n11 = position2 - position
						position = position2
						local magnitude = Vector3.new(n11.X, 0, n11.Z).Magnitude

						if magnitude > 25 and math.abs(n11.Y) < magnitude + 5 and not _G._isTpMoving then
							if _G.antiSwapEnabled then
								local v11 = fn28()

								if v11 then
									task.spawn(function()
										fn33("Body Swap Potion")
									end)
								end
							end

							local flag5 = player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true or tbl20 and os.clock() - tbl20.t < 1.5

							if flag5 then
								task.delay(0.25, function()
									if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
										fn33("Body Swap Potion")
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			fn35(player)

			if player.Character then
				fn36(player.Character)
			end

			player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character2)
				task.wait(0.2)
				fn36(character2)
			end)

			task.spawn(function()
				local tbl25 = { "invers", "jail", "jumpscare", "morph", "rocket", "tiny", "balloon" }
				local v11 = nil

				while true do
					if _G.BJK_HubGen ~= bjkHubGen then
						break
					end
					task.wait(0.5)
					if not frame.Visible then
						v11 = nil
						continue
					end
					local v12 = nil

					pcall(function()
						if not player.Character then
							return
						end

						for i, descendant in ipairs(player.Character.GetDescendants(player.Character)) do
							if descendant:IsA("BillboardGui") and descendant.Enabled and not isOurOverheadBillboard(descendant) then
								for i2, descendant2 in ipairs(descendant:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant2:IsA("TextLabel") and descendant2.Visible ~= false then
										local str = tostring(descendant2.Text):lower()

										for i3, v13 in ipairs(tbl25) do
											if str:find(v13, 1, true) then
												v12 = v13
												return
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)

					if v12 and v12 ~= v11 then
						v11 = v12
						local str = v12 == "invers" and "inverse" or v12
						fn33(str:sub(1, 1):upper() .. str:sub(2) .. " (Admin Panel)")
					elseif not v12 then
						v11 = nil
					end
				end
			end)

			pcall(function()
				player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
					if player:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
						return
					end

					if not (tbl20 and os.clock() - tbl20.t < 2) then
						return
					end

					if os.clock() - 0 < 0.6 then
						return
					end
					local humanoid2 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if humanoid2 and humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
						return
					end
					local flag5 = false

					pcall(function()
						flag5 = _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId and _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId() == player.UserId or false
					end)

					if flag5 then
						return
					end

					if tbl21 and os.clock() - tbl21.t < 6 then
						fn33("Boogie Bomb", tbl21.plr, "Boogie Bomb", tbl21.dist)
					end
				end)
			end)

			tbl23 = {
				{ kw = "boogie", name = "Boogie Bomb" },
				{ kw = "body swap", name = "Body Swap Potion" },
				{ kw = "bodyswap", name = "Body Swap Potion" },
				{ kw = "trap", name = "Trap" },
				{ kw = "subspace", name = "Subspace Mine" },
				{ kw = "beehive", name = "Beehive" },
				{ kw = "sentry", name = "All-Seeing Sentry" },
				{ kw = "rage table", name = "Rage Table" },
				{ kw = "gummy", name = "Gummy Slap" },
				{ kw = "medusa", name = "Medusa's Head" },
			}

			Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				local str = descendant.Name:lower()
				local name = nil

				for i, v11 in ipairs(tbl23) do
					if str:find(v11.kw, 1, true) then
						name = v11.name
						break
					end
				end

				if not name then
					return
				end

				task.defer(function()
					pcall(function()
						local humanoidRootPart2 = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if not humanoidRootPart2 then
							return
						end
						local position

						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							position = descendant.Position
						elseif descendant:IsA("Model") then
							position = descendant:GetPivot().Position
						else
							return
						end

						if (position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude > 60 then
							return
						end

						for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
							if player_.Character and descendant:IsDescendantOf(player_.Character) then
								return
							end
						end

						fn33(name)
					end)
				end)
			end)
		end)

			"Menu",

		local tbl16 = { ["Anti Ragdoll"] = true, ["Infinite Jump"] = true, ["Carpet Speed"] = true }

		function getActionOrder()
			local actionOrder = mainConfig.ActionOrder

			if type(actionOrder) ~= "table" or #actionOrder == 0 then
				actionOrder = nil
			end

			if not actionOrder then
				actionOrder = {}

				for i, v11 in ipairs(tbl15) do
					actionOrder[#actionOrder + 1] = v11
				end
			end

			local tbl17 = {}

			for k in pairs(actionConfig) do
				if not tbl16[k] then
					tbl17[k] = true
				end
			end

			local tbl18 = { MENU = "Menu" }
			local actionOrder2 = {}

			for i, v11 in ipairs(actionOrder) do
				if tbl18[v11] then
					v11 = tbl18[v11]
				end

				if tbl17[v11] then
					actionOrder2[#actionOrder2 + 1] = v11
				end
			end

			for i, v11 in ipairs(tbl15) do
				local flag4 = false

				for i2, v12 in ipairs(actionOrder2) do
					if v12 == v11 then
						flag4 = true
						break
					end
				end

				if not flag4 then
					actionOrder2[#actionOrder2 + 1] = v11
				end
			end

			mainConfig.ActionOrder = actionOrder2
			return actionOrder2
		end

		function moveActionOrder(arg, arg2)
			local v11 = getActionOrder()
			local n10 = arg + arg2

			mainConfig.ActionOrder = v11
			local tbl17 = {}
			saveConfig()
			rebuildActions()
			rebuildActionSettings()
		end

			function spamPlayerBaseOwner(arg)
				if not arg then
					return 0
				end
				local spamBaseOwnerOrder = mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerOrder or AP_ALL_COMMANDS

				if mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerSingleCommand then
					local spamBaseOwnerIndex = _G.SpamBaseOwnerIndex or 1

					if spamBaseOwnerIndex > #spamBaseOwnerOrder then
						spamBaseOwnerIndex = 1
					end

					local v13 = nil
					local n10 = 0

					while n10 < #spamBaseOwnerOrder do
						local n11 = (spamBaseOwnerIndex - 1 + n10) % #spamBaseOwnerOrder + 1
						local v14 = spamBaseOwnerOrder[n11]

						if mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands and mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands[v14] and not apIsOnCooldown(v14) then
							v13 = v14
							_G.SpamBaseOwnerIndex = n11 + 1
							break
						end

						n10 = n10 + 1
					end

					if v13 then
						runAdminCommand(arg, v13)
						return 1
					end
					return 0
				else
					local tbl18 = {}

					for i, v13 in ipairs(spamBaseOwnerOrder) do
						if mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands and mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands[v13] and not apIsOnCooldown(v13) then
							table.insert(tbl18, v13)
						end
					end

					task.spawn(function()
						for i, v13 in ipairs(tbl18) do
							if not arg or not arg.Parent then
								break
							end
							runAdminCommand(arg, v13)

							if i < #tbl18 then
								task.wait(0.01)
							end
						end
					end)

					return #tbl18
				end
			end

			function spamBaseOwner()
				local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
				local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "No character found")
					return
				end
				local v13 = nil
				local huge = math.huge
				local plotsFolder = _G.PlotsFolder or Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

				if plotsFolder then
					for i, child in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
						local plotSign = child:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

						if plotSign then
							local yourBase = plotSign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")

							if not yourBase or not yourBase.Enabled then
								local position = plotSign:IsA("BasePart") and plotSign.Position or plotSign.PrimaryPart and plotSign.PrimaryPart.Position

								if not position then
									local basePart = plotSign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
									position = basePart and basePart.Position
								end

								if position then
									local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position).Magnitude

									if magnitude < huge then
										huge = magnitude
										v13 = child
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not v13 then
					ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "No nearby base found")
					return
				end
				local v14 = nil
				local ok, result = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Packages"):WaitForChild("Synchronizer"))

				if ok and result then
					local v15 = _G.XenSyncGet(v13.Name)

					if v15 then
						local Owner = v15:Get("Owner")

						if Owner then
							if typeof(Owner) == "Instance" and Owner:IsA("Player") then
								v14 = Owner
							elseif type(Owner) == "table" and Owner.Name then
								v14 = Players2:FindFirstChild(Owner.Name)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not v14 then
					local plotSign = v13:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
					local textLabel = plotSign and plotSign:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui") and plotSign.SurfaceGui:FindFirstChild("Frame") and plotSign.SurfaceGui.Frame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")

					if textLabel then
						local text = textLabel.Text
						local match = text and text:match("^(.-)'") or text

						if match then
							for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
								if player_.DisplayName == match or player_.Name == match then
									v14 = player_
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not v14 or v14 == localPlayer2 then
					ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Owner not found or is you")
					return
				end
				ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Spamming " .. v14.DisplayName)
				local v15 = spamPlayerBaseOwner(v14)
				ShowNotification("SPAM OWNER", "Sent " .. tostring(v15) .. " commands")
			end

					refreshTargetPanel = function()

									return false

								return (arg.mpsValue or 0) > (arg2.mpsValue or 0)
							end)

										table.remove(v14, i)
										table.insert(v14, math.min(4, #v14), v15)

						textButton2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 30)

						textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(59, 130, 246)

						textButton2.LayoutOrder = #RP_BUTTONS + 1
						textButton2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
						textButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						textButton2.ZIndex = 13
						textButton2.Parent = frame5
						corner(textButton2, 6)

						textButton2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
							if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
								textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 150, 255)
							end
						end)

						textButton2.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
							if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
								textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(59, 130, 246)
							end
						end)

						textButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
							if isPlayerBlacklisted(arg) then
								ShowNotification("BLOCKED", arg.DisplayName .. " is blacklisted")
								return
							end

							task.spawn(function()
								local tbl19 = {}
								local spamBaseOwnerOrder = mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerOrder or AP_ALL_COMMANDS

								for i, v13 in ipairs(spamBaseOwnerOrder) do
									if mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands and mainConfig.SpamBaseOwnerCommands[v13] and not apIsOnCooldown(v13) then
										table.insert(tbl19, v13)
									end
								end

								if #tbl19 == 0 then
									ShowNotification("ADMIN PANEL", "No cmds available or all on cooldown")
									return
								end

								for i, v13 in ipairs(tbl19) do
									runAdminCommand(arg, v13)
									task.wait(0.01)
								end

								ShowNotification("ADMIN PANEL", "Fired " .. #tbl19 .. " cmds on " .. arg.DisplayName)
							end)
						end)
					end

					local function fn28()
						local v13 = isPlayerBlacklisted(arg)

						if v13 then
							imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0
							textLabel.TextTransparency = 0
							textLabel2.TextTransparency = 0
							textLabel3.TextTransparency = 0

							for i, child in ipairs(frame5:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("TextButton") then
									child.TextTransparency = 0.8
								end
							end
						else
							local tbl18 = {}
							imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0
							textLabel.TextTransparency = 0
							textLabel2.TextTransparency = 0
							textLabel3.TextTransparency = 0

							for i, child in ipairs(frame5:GetChildren()) do
								if child:IsA("TextButton") then
									child.TextTransparency = 0
								end
							end
						end
					end

					fn28()

					task.spawn(function()
						while frame3.Parent do
							task.wait(0.5)
							if not arg or not arg.Parent then
								break
							end
							local v13 = stealLabels[arg.UserId]
							if not v13 then
								break
							end
							local v14, v15 = getStealingInfo(arg)
							local v16, v17, v18, v19 = getHubUserBadge(arg)
							local v20 = _G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId()

							pcall(function()
								if v19 then
									textLabel.RichText = true
									local tbl18 = {}

									for i, v21 in ipairs(v19) do
										local str = string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(v21.color.R * 255), math.floor(v21.color.G * 255), math.floor(v21.color.B * 255))
										tbl18[#tbl18 + 1] = "<font color=\"" .. str .. "\"><b>" .. v21.badge .. "</b></font>"
									end

									textLabel.Text = arg.DisplayName .. "  " .. table.concat(tbl18, " ")
								elseif textLabel.RichText then
									textLabel.RichText = false
									textLabel.Text = arg.DisplayName
								end
							end)

							local flag4 = v20 and v20 == arg.UserId
							local str = nil

							if v18 then
								local str2 = v18:lower()

								for i, v21 in ipairs({ "invers", "jail", "jumpscare", "morph", "rocket", "tiny", "balloon" }) do
									if str2:find(v21, 1, true) then
										str = v21:sub(1, 1):upper() .. v21:sub(2)

										if str == "Invers" then
											str = "Inversed"
										end

										break
									end
								end
							end

							if v15 then
								v13.Text = "\226\151\143 Stealing : " .. tostring(v15)
								v13.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
								frame3.LayoutOrder = 1

								v13.Text = str and "\226\151\143 Base Owner | " .. str or "\226\151\143 Base Owner"
								v13.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 230, 120)
								frame3.LayoutOrder = 2

								v13.Text = "\226\151\143 " .. str
								v13.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 70)
								frame3.LayoutOrder = 10
							else
								v13.Text = ""
								frame3.LayoutOrder = 10
							end

							pcall(function()
								local v21 = getHeldTool(arg)

								if v21 and DANGER_TOOLS[v21] then
									textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
								else
									textLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
								end
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= player then
						createAPRow(player_)
					end
				end

				Players2.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player_)
					task.defer(function()
						createAPRow(player_)
					end)
				end)

				Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player_)
					local v13 = apRows[player_.UserId]

					if v13 then
						v13:Destroy()
						apRows[player_.UserId] = nil
					end

					stealLabels[player_.UserId] = nil
				end)
			end)

			function styleSidebarTabs(arg)
				for k, v13 in pairs(tabButtons) do
					local visible = k == arg
					v13.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SidebarActive or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65)
					v13.BackgroundTransparency = visible and (UITransparency.SidebarTab or 0.12) or 0.82
					v13.TextColor3 = visible and (Theme.SidebarActiveText or Theme.Accent or Theme.Text) or (Theme.SidebarInactiveText or Theme.Text)
					v13.Font = visible and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.GothamMedium
					local activeInd = v13:FindFirstChild("ActiveInd")

					if activeInd then
						activeInd.Visible = visible
						activeInd.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
					end
				end
			end

			_G.styleSidebarTabs = styleSidebarTabs

			tabGroups = {
				{ label = "MAIN", tabs = { "Auto TP", "Priority" } },
				{ label = "VISUALS", tabs = { "ESP", "UI" } },
				{ label = "ADDITIONAL", tabs = { "Misc", "Keybinds" } },
				{ label = "GLOBAL", tabs = { "Performance" } },
			}

			do
				local tbl18 = { "Keybinds", "Auto TP", "ESP", "UI", "Misc", "Priority", "Performance" }

				for i, v13 in ipairs(tabGroups) do
					for i2, tab in ipairs(v13.tabs) do
						local flag4 = false

						for i3, v14 in ipairs(tbl18) do
							if v14 == tab then
								flag4 = true
								break
							end
						end

						if not flag4 then
							table.insert(tbl18, tab)
						end
					end
				end

				for i, v13 in ipairs(tbl18) do
					local flag4 = false

					for i2, v14 in ipairs(tabGroups) do
						for i3, tab in ipairs(v14.tabs) do
							if tab == v13 then
								flag4 = true
								break
							end
						end

						if flag4 then
							break
						end
					end

					if not flag4 then
						continue
					end
					local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
					textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26)
					textButton.LayoutOrder = i
					local uiFlexItem = Instance.new("UIFlexItem")
					uiFlexItem.FlexMode = Enum.UIFlexMode.Fill
					uiFlexItem.Parent = textButton
					textButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SidebarActive or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65)
					textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
					textButton.Text = v13
					textButton.TextColor3 = Theme.SidebarInactiveText or Theme.Text
					textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
					textButton.TextSize = 10
					textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
					textButton.Parent = sidebarNav
					corner(textButton, 20)

					uiPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
					uiPadding.Parent = textButton
					textButton.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
					local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame.Name = "ActiveInd"
					frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
					frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, -6)
					frame.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
					frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame.Visible = false
					frame.Parent = textButton
					corner(frame, 3)

					textButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
						if UI.CurrentTab ~= v13 then
							tw(textButton, { TextColor3 = Theme.Text, BackgroundTransparency = 0.65 }, 0.1)
						end
					end)

					textButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
						if UI.CurrentTab ~= v13 then
							tw(textButton, {
								TextColor3 = Theme.SidebarInactiveText or Theme.Text,
								BackgroundTransparency = 0.85,
							}, 0.1)
						end
					end)

					tabButtons[v13] = textButton
				end
			end

			function makeToolDropdown(parent, text, arg, arg2, arg3)
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 36)
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				frame.Parent = parent
				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 1, 0)
				textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel.Text = text
				textLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
				textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				textLabel.TextSize = 12
				textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				textLabel.Parent = frame
				local v13 = arg2 or arg[1]
				local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
				textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.56, -4, 0, 26)
				textButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.44, 0, 0.5, -13)
				textButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg
				textButton.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Input
				textButton.Text = v13
				textButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
				textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				textButton.TextSize = 10
				textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
				textButton.Parent = frame
				corner(textButton, 8)
				stroke(textButton, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.65)
				local frame2, connection_ = nil, nil

				local function fn27()
					if connection_ then
						connection_:Disconnect()
						connection_ = nil
					end

					if frame2 then
						local v14 = frame2
						v14:Destroy()
						frame2 = nil

						for i, v15 in ipairs(openToolDropdowns) do
							if v15 == v14 then
								table.remove(openToolDropdowns, i)
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end

				local function fn28(text2)
					v13 = text2
					textButton.Text = text2
				end

				local function fn29()
					fn27()
					if not main then
						return
					end
					local absolutePosition = textButton.AbsolutePosition
					local x = textButton.AbsoluteSize.X
					frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(x, #arg * 28 + 4)
					local tbl18 = {}
					frame2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(absolutePosition.X, absolutePosition.Y + textButton.AbsoluteSize.Y + 2)
					frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.InputBg
					frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame2.ZIndex = 200
					frame2.Parent = gui_sg
					openToolDropdowns[#openToolDropdowns + 1] = frame2
					corner(frame2, 6)
					stroke(frame2, Theme.Stroke, 1, 0.35)

					for i, v14 in ipairs(arg) do
						local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")
						textButton2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 26)
						textButton2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, (i - 1) * 28 + 2)
						textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = v14 == v13 and (Theme.SidebarActive or Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 247)) or Theme.InputBg
						textButton2.Text = v14
						textButton2.TextColor3 = v14 == v13 and (Theme.SidebarActiveText or Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)) or Theme.Text
						textButton2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
						textButton2.TextSize = 9
						textButton2.AutoButtonColor = false
						textButton2.ZIndex = 201
						textButton2.Parent = frame2
						corner(textButton2, 4)

						textButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
							fn28(v14)

							if arg3 then
								arg3(v14)
							end

							fn27()
						end)
					end

					connection_ = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
						if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
							return
						end
						local position = input.Position
						local flag4 = frame2 and position.X >= frame2.AbsolutePosition.X and position.X <= frame2.AbsolutePosition.X + frame2.AbsoluteSize.X and position.Y >= frame2.AbsolutePosition.Y and position.Y <= frame2.AbsolutePosition.Y + frame2.AbsoluteSize.Y
						local flag5 = position.X >= absolutePosition.X and position.X <= absolutePosition.X + x and position.Y >= absolutePosition.Y and position.Y <= absolutePosition.Y + textButton.AbsoluteSize.Y

						if not flag4 and not flag5 then
							fn27()
						end
					end)
				end

				textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if frame2 then
						fn27()
					else
						fn29()
					end
				end)

				return fn28
			end

			_allAnimalNames = nil

			getAllAnimalNames = function()
				if _allAnimalNames and #_allAnimalNames > 0 then
					return _allAnimalNames
				end
				_allAnimalNames = {}

				pcall(function()
					local animations = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Animations")
					animations = animations and animations:FindFirstChild("Animals")

					if not animations then
						local models = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Models")
						animations = models and models:FindFirstChild("Animals")
					end

					if animations then
						local tbl18 = {}

						for i, child in ipairs(animations:GetChildren()) do
							if not tbl18[child.Name] then
								tbl18[child.Name] = true
								table.insert(_allAnimalNames, child.Name)
							end
						end

						table.sort(_allAnimalNames, function(arg, arg2)
							return arg:lower() < arg2:lower()
						end)
					end
				end)

				return _allAnimalNames
			end

			task.spawn(getAllAnimalNames)
			_priDragState = { active = false, fromIndex = nil, ghostFrame = nil, overlay = nil }

			cleanupPriDrag = function()
				if _priDragState.ghostFrame then
					pcall(function()
						_priDragState.ghostFrame:Destroy()
					end)

					_priDragState.ghostFrame = nil
				end

				if _priDragState.overlay then
					pcall(function()
						_priDragState.overlay:Destroy()
					end)

					_priDragState.overlay = nil
				end

				_priDragState.active = false
				_priDragState.fromIndex = nil
			end

			makePriorityRow = function(layoutOrder)

				textButton4.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
				textButton4.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Toggle
				textButton4.Text = "\226\150\188"
				textButton4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)
				textButton4.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				textButton4.TextSize = 10
				textButton4.Parent = frame
				corner(textButton4, 5)
				textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
				textButton.Name = "WhiteTextBtn"
				textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 22)
				textButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0.5, -11)
				textButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Red
				textButton.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Toggle
				textButton.Text = "X"
				textButton.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				textButton.TextSize = 10
				textButton.Parent = frame

					if layoutOrder > 1 then

						saveConfig()
						loadTab("Priority")

				end)

				textButton4.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if layoutOrder < #priorityList then

						priorityList[layoutOrder], priorityList[layoutOrder + 1] = priorityList[layoutOrder + 1], priorityList[layoutOrder]
						mainConfig.PriorityList = priorityList
						saveConfig()
						loadTab("Priority")
					end
				end)

				textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

					table.remove(priorityList, layoutOrder)

					if not mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority then
						mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority = {}
					end

					local flag4 = false

					for i, v14 in ipairs(mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority) do
						if v14 == v13 then
							flag4 = true
							break
						end
					end

					if not flag4 then
						table.insert(mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority, v13)
					end

					local tbl18 = {}
					mainConfig.PriorityList = priorityList
					saveConfig()
					loadTab("Priority")
				end)

				textButton2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
				textButton2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textButton2.Text = ""
				textButton2.ZIndex = 9
				textButton2.Parent = frame

				textButton2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)

						return
					end
					cleanupPriDrag()
					_priDragState.active = true
					_priDragState.fromIndex = layoutOrder
					local frame2, connection_, connection_2
					frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, frame.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 31)
					frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
					frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
					frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
					frame2.ZIndex = 100
					frame2.Parent = gui_sg
					corner(frame2, 6)
					local textLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
					textLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					textLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					textLabel3.Text = tostring(layoutOrder) .. ". " .. priorityList[layoutOrder]
					textLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)
					textLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					textLabel3.TextSize = 10
					textLabel3.Parent = frame2
					_priDragState.ghostFrame = frame2

					connection_ = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input2)
						if input2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
							frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0, input2.Position.X - frame2.AbsoluteSize.X / 2, 0, input2.Position.Y - 15)
						end
					end)

					connection_2 = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input2)
						if input2.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input2.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
							return
						end
						connection_.Disconnect(connection_)
						connection_2.Disconnect(connection_2)
						if not _priDragState.active then
							cleanupPriDrag()
							return
						end
						local y = input2.Position.Y
						local n10 = #priorityList

						for i, child in ipairs(mainBody:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Frame") and child.LayoutOrder and child.LayoutOrder >= 1 and child.LayoutOrder <= #priorityList then
								local y2 = child.AbsolutePosition.Y
								local y3 = child.AbsoluteSize.Y
								if y < y2 + y3 / 2 then
									n10 = child.LayoutOrder
									break
								end
							end
						end

						cleanupPriDrag()

						if _priDragState.fromIndex and n10 and _priDragState.fromIndex ~= n10 then
							local v13 = table.remove(priorityList, _priDragState.fromIndex)

							if n10 > _priDragState.fromIndex then
								n10 = n10 - 1
							end

							n10 = math.clamp(n10, 1, #priorityList + 1)
							table.insert(priorityList, n10, v13)
							mainConfig.PriorityList = priorityList
							saveConfig()
							loadTab("Priority")
						end
					end)
				end)
			end

			function makePriorityAddRow()
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 31)

				fn27 = function(arg)
					local match = arg:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
					if not match or match == "" then
						return
					end
					local flag4 = false

					for i, v13 in ipairs(priorityList) do
						if v13 == match then
							flag4 = true
							break
						end
					end

					if not flag4 then
						table.insert(priorityList, match)

						if mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority then
							for i = #mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority, 1, -1 do
								if mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority[i] == match then
									table.remove(mainConfig.RemovedFromPriority, i)
								end
							end
						end

						mainConfig.PriorityList = priorityList
						saveConfig()
					end

					textBox.Text = ""
					frame2.Visible = false
					loadTab("Priority")
				end

				fn28 = function(arg)
					for i, child in ipairs(scrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("TextButton") then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end

					if not arg or arg == "" then
						frame2.Visible = false
						return
					end
					local v13 = getAllAnimalNames()
					local str = arg:lower()
					local tbl18 = {}

					for i, v14 in ipairs(v13) do
						if v14:lower():find(str, 1, true) then
							local flag4 = false

							for i2, v15 in ipairs(priorityList) do
								if v15 == v14 then
									flag4 = true
									break
								end
							end

							if not flag4 then
								table.insert(tbl18, v14)
								if #tbl18 >= 8 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if #tbl18 == 0 then
						frame2.Visible = false
						return
					end
					frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, math.min(#tbl18, 6) * 24)
					frame2.Visible = true

					for i, v14 in ipairs(tbl18) do
						local textButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")
						textButton2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
						textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row
						textButton2.BackgroundTransparency = UITransparency.Dropdown
						textButton2.Text = v14
						textButton2.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
						textButton2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
						textButton2.TextSize = 10
						textButton2.AutoButtonColor = false
						textButton2.ZIndex = 52
						textButton2.Parent = scrollingFrame

						textButton2.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
							textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.RowHover
						end)

						textButton2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
							textButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Row
						end)

						textButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
							fn27(v14)
						end)
					end
				end

				textBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
					fn28(textBox.Text)
				end)

				textBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
					task.delay(0.15, function()
						frame2.Visible = false
					end)
				end)

				textBox.Focused:Connect(function()
					if textBox.Text ~= "" then
						fn28(textBox.Text)
					end
				end)

				textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					fn27(textBox.Text)
				end)
			end

			if not _G.BJK_FaceAwaySet then
				local RunService_ = game:GetService("RunService")

				local tbl18 = {
					ligado = false,
					roubando = false,
					travado = nil,
					autoRot = nil,

				local v13, v14, tbl19 = nil, nil, {}

				local function fn27(arg)
					local character2 = arg and arg.Character
					return character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or nil
				end

				local function fn28()
					if type(_G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId) ~= "function" then
						return nil
					end
					local ok, result = pcall(_G.__getCurrentBaseOwnerId)

					if ok and result then
						local playerByUserId = Players2:GetPlayerByUserId(result)
						if playerByUserId and playerByUserId ~= localPlayer2 then
							return playerByUserId
						end
					end

					return nil
				end

				local function fn29()
					local v16 = fn27(localPlayer2)
					if not v16 then
						return nil
					end
					local v17, huge = nil, math.huge

					for i, player_ in ipairs(Players2:GetPlayers()) do
						if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
							local v18 = fn27(player_)

							if v18 then
								local magnitude = (v18.Position - v16.Position).Magnitude

								if magnitude < huge then
									huge, v17 = magnitude, player_
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return v17
				end

				local function fn30()
					local faceAwayModo = mainConfig.FaceAwayModo or "baseowner"
					if faceAwayModo == "nearest" then
						return fn29()
					end

					if faceAwayModo == "player" then
						return mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo and Players2:FindFirstChild(mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo) or nil
					end

					if tbl18.roubando then
						if tbl18.travado and tbl18.travado.Parent then
							return tbl18.travado
						end
						local v16 = fn28()
						if v16 then
							tbl18.travado = v16
							return v16
						end
						return nil
					end

					return fn28()
				end

				local function fn31()
					local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
					local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

					if humanoid2 and tbl18.autoRot ~= nil then
						pcall(function()
							humanoid2.AutoRotate = tbl18.autoRot
						end)
					end

					tbl18.autoRot = nil
				end

				local function fn32(arg)
					local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
					if not character2 then
						return false
					end
					local humanoidRootPart2 = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if not humanoidRootPart2 then
						return false
					end

					if humanoid2 and tbl18.autoRot == nil then
						tbl18.autoRot = humanoid2.AutoRotate

						pcall(function()
							humanoid2.AutoRotate = false
						end)
					end

					local position = humanoidRootPart2.Position
					local n10 = arg - position
					n10 = Vector3.new(n10.X, 0, n10.Z)
					if n10.Magnitude < 0.05 then
						return false
					end
					n10 = n10.Unit
					humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(position, position - n10 * 10)
					return true
				end

				local function fn33()
					if highlight then
						highlight.Enabled = false
					end

					if billboardGui then
						billboardGui.Enabled = false
					end

					v15 = nil
				end

					if not workspace.CurrentCamera then

				local function fn35(arg, arg2)
					if not arg or not mainConfig.FaceAway then
						fn33()
						return
					end

					if not fn34() then
						return
					end
					local character2 = arg.Character
					if not character2 then
						fn33()
						return
					end

					if v15 ~= arg or highlight.Adornee ~= character2 then
						v15 = arg
						highlight.Adornee = character2
						billboardGui.Adornee = character2:FindFirstChild("Head") or character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					end

					highlight.Enabled = true
					billboardGui.Enabled = billboardGui.Adornee ~= nil

					if textLabel5 then
						local text = (tbl18.roubando and "FACING AWAY  " or "TARGET  ") .. (arg2 or 0) .. "m"

						if textLabel5.Text ~= text then
							textLabel5.Text = text
						end
					end
				end

				local function fn36()
					local faceAwayModo = mainConfig.FaceAwayModo or "baseowner"

					for i, v16 in ipairs(tbl19) do
						local flag4

						if v16.kind == "player" then
							flag4 = faceAwayModo == "player" and mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo == v16.playerName
						else
							flag4 = faceAwayModo == v16.kind
						end

						v16.dot.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0 or 1
						v16.stroke.Color = flag4 and Theme.Accent or Theme.Stroke
						v16.stroke.Transparency = flag4 and 0.1 or 0.62
						v16.frame.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.15 or 0.45
					end
				end

				end

					textLabel4.Parent = frame
					corner(textLabel4, 7)
					fn39()

					Players2.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
						task.defer(fn39)
					end)

					Players2.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player_)
						if mainConfig.FaceAwayModo == "player" and mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo == player_.Name then
							mainConfig.FaceAwayModo = "baseowner"
							mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo = nil
							mainConfig.FaceAwayNearest = false
							tbl18.travado = nil
							tbl18.alvoCache = nil
						end

						task.defer(fn39)
					end)

					pcall(function()
						applySavedPosition("Sticky loves u\nFace Away", v13)
					end)

					makeDraggable(v13, v13:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame") and v13:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame"):FindFirstChild("TextLabel"), "Sticky loves u\nFace Away")
					return true
				end

				local function fn41(arg, arg2)
					if not v13 or not v13.Visible then
						return
					end
					local faceAwayModo = mainConfig.FaceAwayModo or "baseowner"
					local str = faceAwayModo == "nearest" and "Nearest Player" or faceAwayModo == "player" and "@" .. tostring(mainConfig.FaceAwayAlvo) or "Base Owner"
					local str2 = faceAwayModo == "baseowner" and tbl18.travado and "  <font color=\"#38D66E\">[locked]</font>" or ""
					textLabel2.Text = str .. str2

					if arg then
						if textLabel.Text ~= arg.DisplayName then
							textLabel.Text = arg.DisplayName
							imageLabel.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. arg.UserId .. "&w=48&h=48"
						end

						textLabel3.Text = tostring(arg2 or 0) .. " studs"

						if tbl18.roubando then
							textLabel4.Text = "FACING AWAY"
							textLabel4.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Green
							textLabel4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
						else
							textLabel4.Text = "ARMED  -  not stealing"
							textLabel4.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SoftAccent
							textLabel4.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
						end
					else
						textLabel.Text = "No target"
						imageLabel.Image = ""
						local tbl20 = {}
						textLabel3.Text = "--"
						textLabel4.Text = "NO TARGET"
						textLabel4.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ToggleOff2
						textLabel4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					end
				end

				local function fn42()
					fn40()

					if v13 then
						local faceAway = mainConfig.Visibilities and mainConfig.Visibilities["Face Away"]

						if faceAway ~= false then
							openAnim(v13)
						end
					end

					if tbl18.conn then
						return
					end

					tbl18.conn = RunService_.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						if not tbl18.ligado then
							return
						end
						local roubando = localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") and true or false

						if roubando ~= tbl18.roubando then
							tbl18.roubando = roubando

							if not roubando then
								fn31()
								tbl18.travado = nil
							end
						end

						local now = os.clock()

						if now - tbl18.ultimoAlvo >= 0.25 then
							tbl18.ultimoAlvo = now
							tbl18.alvoCache = fn30()
						end

						local alvoCache = tbl18.alvoCache
						local v16 = alvoCache and fn27(alvoCache) or nil
						local v17 = fn27(localPlayer2)
						local n10 = 0

						if v16 and v17 then
							n10 = math.floor((v16.Position - v17.Position).Magnitude + 0.5)
						end

						if roubando and v16 then
							fn32(v16.Position)
						end

						fn35(alvoCache, n10)
						fn41(alvoCache, n10)
					end)
				end

				local fn43

				fn43 = function()
					if tbl18.conn then
						tbl18.conn:Disconnect()
						tbl18.conn = nil
					end

					local tbl20 = {}
					fn31()
					tbl18.travado = nil
					fn33()

					if v13 then
						closeAnim(v13)
					end
				end

				_G.BJK_FaceAwaySet = function(arg)
					tbl18.ligado = arg and true or false

					if tbl18.ligado then
						fn42()
					else
						fn43()
					end

					mainConfig.FaceAway = tbl18.ligado
					saveConfig()
				end

				_G.BJK_FaceAwayOn = function()
					return tbl18.ligado
				end

				if mainConfig.FaceAway then
					tbl18.ligado = true
					fn42()
				end
			end

							saveConfig()
						end

							Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = fov

					textLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

					textLabel2.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom

					frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

					uiListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
					uiListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
					uiListLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
					uiListLayout.Parent = frame

						"#4A8CFF",
						"#AA55FF",

			function hexToColor3(arg)
				if typeof(arg) == "Color3" then
					return arg
				end

				if type(arg) ~= "string" then
					return nil
				end
				arg = arg:gsub("#", ""):gsub("%s", "")

				if #arg == 3 then
					arg = arg:sub(1, 1):rep(2) .. arg:sub(2, 2):rep(2) .. arg:sub(3, 3):rep(2)
				end

				if #arg ~= 6 or arg:find("%X") then
					return nil
				end
				return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(arg:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(arg:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(arg:sub(5, 6), 16))
			end

			setMenuAccent = function(arg)
				local v13 = hexToColor3(arg)
				if not v13 then
					return false
				end
				Themes.Nexus.Accent = v13
				local n10
				Themes.Nexus.ToggleOn = v13
				Themes.Nexus.SliderFill = v13
				Themes.Nexus.SidebarActiveText = v13
				n10 = 0.299 * v13.R + 0.587 * v13.G + 0.114 * v13.B

				if n10 > 0.5 then
					Themes.Nexus.AccentLight = v13:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 0.3)
					Themes.Nexus.SoftAccent = v13:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 26), 0.55)
					Themes.Nexus.SoftAccentHover = v13:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 26), 0.45)
				else
					Themes.Nexus.SoftAccent = v13:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 26), 0.78)
					Themes.Nexus.SoftAccentHover = v13:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 26), 0.68)
				end

				Themes.Nexus.ToggleKnobOn = n10 > 0.7 and Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

				if mainConfig then
					mainConfig.AccentHex = type(arg) == "string" and arg or mainConfig.AccentHex

					if saveConfig then
						saveConfig()
					end
				end

				_G._currentTheme = nil
				pcall(applyTheme, "Nexus")

				pcall(function()
					if _G.applyLEDBarColor then
						_G.applyLEDBarColor(v13)
					end
				end)

				return true
			end

			tbl17 = {
				stroke = nil,
				grad = nil,
				conn = nil,
				color = Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255),
			}

			fn26 = function(arg)
				local v13 = arg:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.75)
				local v14 = arg:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.45)

				return ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, v13),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, arg),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, v14),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, arg),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, v13),
				})
			end

			_G.applyLEDBarColor = function(color2)
				if typeof(color2) ~= "Color3" then
					return
				end
				tbl17.color = color2

				if tbl17.grad then
					pcall(function()
						tbl17.grad.Color = fn26(color2)
					end)
				end

				if tbl17.stroke then
					pcall(function()
						tbl17.stroke.Color = color2
					end)
				end
			end

			_G.setLEDBar = function(arg)
				if tbl17.conn then
					tbl17.conn:Disconnect()
					tbl17.conn = nil
				end

				if tbl17.stroke then
					local tbl18 = {}

					pcall(function()
						tbl17.stroke:Destroy()
					end)

					tbl17.stroke = nil
					tbl17.grad = nil
				end

				if not arg then
					return
				end

				if not bottomBar or not bottomBar.Parent then
					return
				end
				local color2 = tbl17.color or Theme.Accent or Color3.fromRGB(74, 140, 255)
				local uiStroke, uiGradient
				uiStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
				uiStroke.Name = "LEDBarStroke"
				uiStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
				uiStroke.Thickness = 5
				uiStroke.Transparency = 0.15
				uiStroke.Color = color2
				uiStroke.Parent = bottomBar
				uiGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
				uiGradient.Color = fn26(color2)
				uiGradient.Rotation = 0
				uiGradient.Parent = uiStroke
				tbl17.stroke = uiStroke
				tbl17.grad = uiGradient
				local now = tick()
				local n10 = 0

				tbl17.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if not uiStroke.Parent or not bottomBar or not bottomBar.Parent then
						if tbl17.conn then
							tbl17.conn:Disconnect()
							tbl17.conn = nil
						end

						return
					end

					local now2 = tick()
					if now2 - n10 < 0.016 then
						return
					end
					n10 = now2
					local x = bottomBar.AbsoluteSize.X
					local y = bottomBar.AbsoluteSize.Y

					if x > 0 and y > 0 then
						local n11 = (x + y) * 2
						local n12 = (now2 - now) % 2.5
						local n13 = n12 / 2.5
						local n14 = n13 * n11 % n11
						local rotation = 0

						if n14 < x then
							rotation = n14 / x * 90
						elseif n14 < x + y then
							rotation = 90 + (n14 - x) / y * 90
						elseif n14 < x * 2 + y then
							rotation = 180 + (n14 - x - y) / x * 90
						else
							rotation = 270 + (n14 - x * 2 - y) / y * 90
						end

						uiGradient.Rotation = rotation
						local v13 = math.sin(n13 * math.pi * 2)
						uiStroke.Transparency = 0.05 + (v13 + 1) * 0.5 * 0.35
						uiStroke.Thickness = 5 + math.sin(now2 * 5) * 0.2
					end
				end)
			end

			applyMenuBackground = function(arg)
				local imageTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(mainConfig and mainConfig.MenuBgOpacity or 65, 0, 100) / 100
				local image = nil

				if arg and arg ~= "" then
					arg = tostring(arg)

					if arg:match("^%d+$") then
						image = "rbxassetid://" .. arg
					elseif arg:match("^rbxasset") then
						image = arg
					elseif arg:match("^http") then
						pcall(function()
							local response = game:HttpGet(arg)
							writefile("bjk_bg_img", response)

							if getcustomasset then
								image = getcustomasset("bjk_bg_img")
							end
						end)
					else
						image = arg
					end
				end

				local tbl18 = { main, actionSettingsPanel, tpSpeedSettingsPanel }

				if panels then
					for k, v13 in pairs(panels) do
						table.insert(tbl18, v13)
					end
				end

				for i, v13 in ipairs(tbl18) do
					if typeof(v13) == "Instance" and v13:IsA("Frame") and v13.Parent == gui then
						local bjkBgImage = v13:FindFirstChild("BJKBgImage")

						if not image then
							if bjkBgImage then
								bjkBgImage:Destroy()
							end
						else
							if not bjkBgImage then
								bjkBgImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
								bjkBgImage.Name = "BJKBgImage"
								bjkBgImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
								bjkBgImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
								bjkBgImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
								bjkBgImage.ZIndex = -5
								bjkBgImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
								local uiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
								uiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
								uiCorner.Parent = bjkBgImage
								bjkBgImage.Parent = v13
							end

							bjkBgImage.Image = image
							bjkBgImage.ImageTransparency = imageTransparency
						end

						local flag4 = image ~= nil

						if v13 == main then
							local sidebar = v13:FindFirstChild("Sidebar")

							if sidebar then
								sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.45 or UITransparency.Sidebar
							end

							local contentBg = v13:FindFirstChild("ContentBg")

							if contentBg then
								contentBg.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.5 or UITransparency.Content
							end

							v13.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.35 or UITransparency.MainPanel
						else
							v13.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.4 or UITransparency.Panel
						end
					end
				end
			end

			_G.setMenuAccent = setMenuAccent
			_G.applyMenuBackground = applyMenuBackground

			UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
				if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					return
				end

					return
				end
				local name = input.KeyCode.Name
				if gameProcessed then
					return
				end

				if input.KeyCode == UI.OpenMenuKey then
					if main.Visible then
						closeAnim(main)
						closeAllSecondaryPanels()
					else
						openAnim(main)
					end

					return
				end

				local name2 = input.KeyCode.Name

				local handlers = {
					Clone = instantClone,
					["Carpet Boost"] = function()
					setCarpetSpeed(not CarpetState.enabled)

					setToggle("Click to AP", mainConfig.ClickToAP)
					setToggle("ClickToAP", mainConfig.ClickToAP)
					ShowNotification("CLICK TO AP", mainConfig.ClickToAP and "Enabled" or "Disabled")
				end,
				}

				for k, v13 in pairs(Keybinds) do
					if v13 == name2 and handlers[k] then
						handlers[k]()
						return
					end
				end
			end)

			RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				if not targetHud or not targetHud.Visible then

				if v22 then
					local tbl18 = {}
					stroke_ = RAG_RED
					glow = RAG_RED
					fILL1 = RAG_RED
					text = RAG_ORANGE
					text2 = RAG_RED

					if v23 and v23 > 0 then
						local attribute = localPlayer2:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")

						if attribute ~= ragLastEndTime then
							ragLastEndTime = attribute
							ragTotalDuration = v23
						end

						local n11 = math.clamp(v23 / math.max(ragTotalDuration or v23, 0.1), 0, 1)
						text3 = string.format("RAGDOLL - %.1fs", v23)
						udim2 = UDim2.new(n11, 0, 1, 0)
						text4 = string.format("%.1fs", v23)
					else

					connection_:Disconnect()
				end)

				connection_ = nil
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(_G._buildUI)
			_G.BJK_Ready = true
			local ok2, result2 = pcall(InicializarRecursos)

			if not ok2 then
			end

			local tbl14 = {}
			_G.TpOnLoadRunning = false
			_G._tpFiring = nil
			_G._bjkUltraTP = nil
		end)
	else
		local ok, result, ok2, result2
		ok, result = pcall(_G._buildUI)
		_G.BJK_Ready = true
		ok2, result2 = pcall(InicializarRecursos)

		if not ok2 then
			task.delay(0.5, function()
				pcall(InicializarRecursos)
			end)
		end
	end

			end

		local function fn33(arg, arg2, arg3)

			if n11 ~= nil then
				return n11
			end
			n11 = 0

				n11 = xenAnimShim:GetGeneration(arg, str ~= "None" and str or nil, arg3)

				if not n11 or n11 == 0 then
					n11 = xenAnimShim:GetGeneration(arg, "None", nil) or 0
				end
			end)
