		end

		local function scanAllPets_()
			local tbl19 = {}
			local n11 = 0
			if not loadModules_() then
				return tbl19, 1
			end
			local plotsFolder = _G.PlotsFolder or workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
			if not plotsFolder then
				return tbl19, 1
			end

			for i, child in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
				local v12 = fn26(child.Name)
				if not v12 then
					n11 = n11 + 1
					continue
				end
				local name
				local flag4 = true

				pcall(function()
					local owner = fn27(v12, "Owner")
					if not owner then
						return
					end

					if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then
						if owner.UserId == localPlayer3.UserId then
							return
						end

						if Players3:FindFirstChild(owner.Name) then
							name = owner.Name
							flag4 = false
						end
					elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.Name then
						if owner.UserId and owner.UserId == localPlayer3.UserId then
							return
						end

						if Players3:FindFirstChild(tostring(owner.Name)) then
							name = owner.Name
							flag4 = false
						end
					elseif type(owner) == "string" then
						if owner ~= localPlayer3.Name and owner ~= localPlayer3.DisplayName and Players3:FindFirstChild(owner) then
							name = owner
							flag4 = false
						end
					elseif type(owner) == "number" then
						if owner ~= localPlayer3.UserId and Players3:GetPlayerByUserId(owner) then
							flag4 = false
						end
					end
				end)

				if flag4 then
					continue
				end
				local animalList = fn27(v12, "AnimalList")
				if not animalList then
					continue
				end

				for k, v13 in pairs(animalList) do
					if type(v13) ~= "table" then
						continue
					end
					local index = v13.Index
					if not index then
						continue
					end
					local v14 = Animals and Animals[index]
					if not v14 then
						n11 = n11 + 1
						continue
					end

					if fn32(v13) then
						continue
					end
					local mutation = v13.Mutation or "None"
					local v15 = fn33(index, mutation, v13.Traits)
					local displayName = v14 and v14.DisplayName or index
					local v16 = fn31(child, k)

					if v16 then
						table.insert(tbl19, {
							name = displayName,
							index = index,
							mps = v15,
							mutation = mutation,
							position = v16,
							plot = child.Name,
							slot = tostring(k),
							owner = name,
						})
					else
						n11 = n11 + 1
					end
				end
			end

			local renderedMovingAnimals = workspace:FindFirstChild("RenderedMovingAnimals")

			if renderedMovingAnimals then
				for i, child in ipairs(renderedMovingAnimals:GetChildren()) do
					pcall(function()
						if not child:IsA("Model") then
							return
						end
						local v12 = Animals and Animals[child.Name]
						if not v12 then
							return
						end
						local attribute = child:GetAttribute("Mutation") or "None"
						local v13 = fn33(child.Name, attribute, nil)
						if v13 <= 0 then
							return
						end
						local primaryPart = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
						local position = primaryPart and primaryPart.Position or child:GetPivot().Position

						table.insert(tbl19, {
							name = v12.DisplayName or child.Name,
							index = child.Name,
							mps = v13,
							mutation = attribute,
							position = position,
							plot = nil,
							slot = nil,
							conveyor = true,
							model = child,
						})
					end)
				end
			end

			table.sort(tbl19, function(arg, arg2)
				return handlers[17](arg.name, arg2.name, arg.mutation, arg2.mutation, arg.mps, arg2.mps)
			end)

			return tbl19, n11
		end

		local function fn39(arg, arg2)

			raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true

					filterDescendantsInstances[#filterDescendantsInstances + 1] = player_.Character
				end
			end

			local n14 = arg
			local n15 = arg3

				if not hit then
					return false
				end
				local instance = hit.Instance
				if instance and instance.CanCollide and not handlers[33][instance.Name] then
					return true
				end
				n15 = n15 - ((hit.Position - n14).Magnitude + 0.3)
				if n15 <= 0 then
					return false
				end
				n14 = hit.Position + unit * 0.3
				filterDescendantsInstances[#filterDescendantsInstances + 1] = instance
			end

			return false
		end

					return true
				end
			end

		local function fn49(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
			if not arg or not arg.Parent or #arg2 == 0 then
				return
			end
			local n14, n15, flag4, connection_, fn50, n16, now, now2, v12, n17, tbl20, n18, position, n19, n20, now3
			n14 = (arg3 or handlers[2].CARPET) / fn46()
			fn44(arg.Position, arg2)
			n15 = 1
			flag4 = false

			fn50 = function()
				if flag4 then
					return
				end
				flag4 = true

				if arg and arg.Parent then
					arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					local v13 = arg2[#arg2]

					if (arg.Position - v13).Magnitude <= 25 then

							k.CanCollide = v13
						end
					end

				end)

				if connection_ then
					connection_.Disconnect(connection_)
				end

			end

			local huge, n21 = math.huge, 0
			n16 = 0
			now = os.clock()
			now2 = os.clock()
			v12 = nil
			n17 = math.floor(18 * fn46())

			if arg5 then
				local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
				local filterDescendantsInstances
				raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true

					if player_.Character then
						filterDescendantsInstances[#filterDescendantsInstances + 1] = player_.Character
					end
				end

					local v13 = arg2[n15]
					if not v13 then
						break
					end
					local vector4 = Vector3.new(v13.X - arg.Position.X, 0, v13.Z - arg.Position.Z)
					local magnitude = vector4.Magnitude
					if magnitude < 1 then
						break
					end
					local n22 = arg.Position + vector4.Unit * math.min(20, magnitude)
					local hit = workspace:Raycast(arg.Position, n22 - arg.Position, raycastParams)
					if hit and hit.Instance and hit.Instance.CanCollide then
						break
					end
					arg.CFrame = arg.CFrame - arg.CFrame.Position + n22
					arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					RunService_.Heartbeat:Wait()
					if not arg or not arg.Parent then
						return
					end
				end
			end

			pcall(function()
				if sethiddenproperty and arg and arg.Parent then
					sethiddenproperty(arg, "NetworkOwnershipRule", 0)
				end
			end)

			tbl20 = {}

			pcall(function()
				local tbl21 = { arg }

				if arg.Parent then
					for i, descendant in ipairs(arg.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							tbl21[#tbl21 + 1] = descendant
						end
					end
				end

				for i, v13 in ipairs(tbl21) do
					tbl20[v13] = v13.CanCollide
				end
			end)

			connection_ = RunService_.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not arg or not arg.Parent or flag4 then
					if connection_ then
						connection_.Disconnect(connection_)
					end

					return
				end

				pcall(function()
					if sethiddenproperty and arg and arg.Parent then
						sethiddenproperty(arg, "NetworkOwnershipRule", 0)
					end
				end)

				pcall(function() end)
				handlers[7]()
				local v13 = arg2[n15]
				local n22 = v13 - arg.Position
				local magnitude = n22.Magnitude
				local n23 = fn45()

				if n15 >= #arg2 then
					n23 = math.max(1.2, fn45() * 0.5)
				end

				local now4 = os.clock()
				local n24 = math.min(0.25, now4 - now2)
				now2 = now4

				if n15 < #arg2 then
					n23 = math.max(n23, n16 * n24 * 1.1)
				end

				if magnitude >= n23 and v12 then
					local v14 = n22.Unit:Dot(v12)

					if v14 < -0.3 then

						if n15 > #arg2 then
							fn50()
							return
						end
						huge, n21 = math.huge, 0
						local tbl21 = {}
						v13 = arg2[n15]
						n22 = v13 - arg.Position
						magnitude = n22.Magnitude
					end
				end

				if magnitude < n23 then
					n15 = n15 + 1
					if n15 > #arg2 then
						fn50()
						return
					end
					huge, n21 = math.huge, 0
					v13 = arg2[n15]
					n22 = v13 - arg.Position
					magnitude = n22.Magnitude
				end

				if magnitude > huge - 0.05 then
					if magnitude > huge + 15 then
						n21 = 0
					else
						n21 = n21 + 1
					end
				else
					n21 = 0
				end

				huge = magnitude
				if n21 >= n17 then
					fn50()
					return
				end

				if magnitude >= 0.1 then
					local unit = n22.Unit
					local vector4 = Vector3.new(unit.X, 0, unit.Z)

					if vector4.Magnitude > 0.001 then
						vector4 = vector4.Unit
						v12 = vector4

						pcall(function()
							if arg and arg.Parent then
								arg.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(arg.Position, arg.Position + vector4)
							end
						end)
					end

					local v14 = n14
					local now5 = os.clock()
					local n25 = math.min(0.1, now5 - now)
					local n26
					now = now5
					n26 = math.abs(v14 - n16) > v14 * 0.6 and 14 or 22
					n16 = n16 + (v14 - n16) * math.min(1, n25 * n26)

					if arg4 and n22.Y > 5 and n15 < #arg2 then
						local humanoid2 = arg.Parent and arg.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if humanoid2 then
							local state = humanoid2:GetState()

							if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
								pcall(function()
									humanoid2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
								end)

								pcall(function()
									humanoid2.Jump = true
								end)
							end
						end
					end

					local n27 = unit.Y * n16

					if n27 > 60 then
						n27 = 60
					end

					arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(unit.X * n16, n27, unit.Z * n16)
				end
			end)

			n18 = 0
			position = arg.Position

			for i, v13 in ipairs(arg2) do
				n18 = n18 + (position - v13).Magnitude
				position = v13
			end

			n19 = (n18 / math.min(200, n14) + 3) * fn47()
			n20 = 0
			now3 = os.clock()

			while not flag4 and n20 < n19 do
				task.wait(0.05)
				n20 = os.clock() - now3
			end

			fn50()
			fn48(arg)
		end

		local tbl20 = {
			Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
			Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
			Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),
			Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),

				return false
			end

					end

		handlers[40] = function(arg)

		end

				local n15 = arg2 - n14
				if n15.Magnitude < 0.05 then
					return nil
				end

				if not hit then
					return nil
				end

				if arg3(hit.Instance) then
					return hit
				end
				filterDescendantsInstances[#filterDescendantsInstances + 1] = hit.Instance
				n14 = hit.Position + n15.Unit * 0.3
			end

			return nil
		end

		handlers[44] = function(arg, arg2)
			return handlers[43](arg, arg2) == nil
		end

		local function fn51(arg)
			if #arg <= 2 then
				return arg
			end

		local function fn53(arg, arg2)

		end

		local function fn54(arg)
			if #arg <= 2 then
				return arg
			end
			local tbl25 = { arg[1] }
			local n14 = 1

		local function fn55(arg)
			if #arg <= 2 then
				return arg
			end
			local tbl25 = { arg[1] }

					end
				else
					tbl25[#tbl25 + 1] = v12
				end
			end

			tbl25[#tbl25 + 1] = arg[#arg]
			return tbl25
		end

		local function fn56(arg, arg2)
			return arg >= tbl23.minX and arg <= tbl23.maxX and arg2 >= tbl23.minZ and arg2 <= tbl23.maxZ
		end

							local n14, n15, n16, n17, n18, n19, n20, n21

		local function fn59(arg, arg2, arg3)
			if not arg or not arg.Parent or #arg2 == 0 then
				return
			end
			local v12 = fn46()
			local n14 = math.max(4, math.floor(15 / v12))
			local n15 = math.max(4, fn45() + 2)
			local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
			local filterDescendantsInstances, n16, position, n17, now
			raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true
			filterDescendantsInstances = {}

			for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
				if player_.Character then
					filterDescendantsInstances[#filterDescendantsInstances + 1] = player_.Character
				end

				if sethiddenproperty and arg and arg.Parent then
					sethiddenproperty(arg, "NetworkOwnershipRule", 0)
				end

			position = arg.Position
			n17 = 0
			now = os.clock()

			while n16 <= #arg2 and arg.Parent and os.clock() - now < 10 * v12 do
				local v13 = arg2[n16]
				local n18 = v13 - arg.Position
				local magnitude = n18.Magnitude

				if magnitude <= n15 then
					n16 = n16 + 1
					n17 = 0
				else
					local n19 = math.min(n14, magnitude)
					local n20 = arg.Position + n18.Unit * n19

					if not arg3 then
						local hit = workspace:Raycast(arg.Position, n20 - arg.Position, raycastParams)

						if hit and hit.Instance and hit.Instance.CanCollide then
							local n21 = arg.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
							local hit2 = workspace:Raycast(n21, n20 - n21, raycastParams)

							else
								n20 = n21
							end
						end
					end

						if not arg or not arg.Parent then
							return
						end
						arg.CFrame = arg.CFrame - arg.CFrame.Position + n20
						arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end)
				end

					n17 = n17 + 1

					if n17 >= math.floor(8 * v12) then

							if arg and arg.Parent then
								local unit = (arg2[n16] - arg.Position).Unit

								arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
								arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
							end
						end)

						n17 = 0
					end
				else
					n17 = 0
				end

			end

			if arg and arg.Parent then

				if (arg.Position - v13).Magnitude <= 25 then

							arg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							arg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

			end
		end

		local function fn60(arg)

		local tbl25 = nil

		local function fn61()
			local tbl26, n14 = nil, 0

			pcall(function()
				for i, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Model") then
						local str = tostring(child.Name or ""):lower()

							local ok, result, result2 = pcall(function()
								return child:GetBoundingBox()
							end)

							if ok and result and result2 then
								local n15 = result2.X * result2.Z

								if n15 > n14 then
									n14 = n15
									tbl26 = { cf = result, size = result2 }
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			if not tbl26 then
				tbl25 = nil
				return nil
			end

			tbl25 = {
				px = tbl26.cf.Position.X,
				pz = tbl26.cf.Position.Z,
				top = tbl26.cf.Position.Y + tbl26.size.Y / 2,
				hx = tbl26.size.X / 2 + 8,
				hz = tbl26.size.Z / 2 + 8,
			}

			return tbl25
		end

		local function fn62(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			arg4 = arg4 or 0
			local n14, n15 = arg3.hx + arg4, arg3.hz + arg4
			local n16, n17 = arg2.X - arg.X, arg2.Z - arg.Z
			local n18, n19 = arg.X - arg3.px, arg.Z - arg3.pz
			local n20, n21 = 0, 1

			if math.abs(n16) < 1e-06 then
				if math.abs(n18) >= n14 then
					return false
				end
			else
				local n22, n23 = (-n14 - n18) / n16, (n14 - n18) / n16

				if n22 > n23 then
					n22, n23 = n23, n22
				end

				n20 = math.max(n20, n22)
				n21 = math.min(n21, n23)
				if n20 > n21 then
					return false
				end
			end

			if math.abs(n17) < 1e-06 then
				if math.abs(n19) >= n15 then
					return false
				end
			else
				local n22, n23 = (-n15 - n19) / n17, (n15 - n19) / n17

				if n22 > n23 then
					n22, n23 = n23, n22
				end

				n20 = math.max(n20, n22)
				n21 = math.min(n21, n23)
				if n20 > n21 then
					return false
				end
			end

			return true
		end

		local function fn63(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local n14 = math.max(arg.Y, arg2.Y)

			local tbl26 = {
				{ arg3.px + arg3.hx, arg3.pz + arg3.hz },
				{ arg3.px + arg3.hx, arg3.pz - arg3.hz },

					if not v12 or n15 < v12 then
						v12, tbl27 = n15, { vector4 }
					end
				end
			end

			return tbl27
		end

				if connection_4 then
					connection_4:Disconnect()
				end

				_G._cloneCompleted = true

				pcall(function()
					local character4, v21, quantumCloner
					character4 = localPlayer3.Character
					v21 = character4 and character4.FindFirstChildOfClass(character4, "Humanoid")
					quantumCloner = character4 and (character4.FindFirstChild(character4, "Quantum Cloner") or localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner"))

						v21.UnequipTools(v21, quantumCloner)
					end

				local humanoidRootPart6 = character4 and character4:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					local ok, result = pcall(scanAllPets_)

					if not ok then
						result = {}
					end

					for i, v21 in ipairs(result) do
						if v21.plot and v21.slot and tostring(v21.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(v21.slot) == tostring(selectedPetData.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(selectedPetData.slot) then
							if not handlers[65](v21) then
								flag12 = true
							end

							break
						end
					end
				end

				if flag12 then
					if connection_ then
						connection_:Disconnect()
					end

					connection_2:Disconnect()

					if humanoidRootPart6 and humanoidRootPart6.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							humanoidRootPart6.Anchored = false
						end)

						fn48(humanoidRootPart6)
					end

					handlers[72] = false
					_G._isTpMoving = false
					return
				end
			end

			if connection_ then
				connection_:Disconnect()
			end

			handlers[72] = false
			_G._isTpMoving = false
			handlers[69] = 0
			n16 = 0
		end

		doGrabbleVelocityTP = handlers[78]

		_G.doGrabbleVelocityTP = handlers[78]

		do

			local n18 = 0

			local function fn76(arg)
				if not arg then
					return nil

					return position

				local character2, v15, flag7, v16

				flag7 = v15 and v15.Health > 0

					v14 = v16

				if not arg then
					return false
				end

				if not plotsFolder then
					return false
				end
				local v15 = getPlotAtPosition(arg)

				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					return false
				end
				local v16 = getPlotAtPosition(humanoidRootPart2.Position)
				return v15 and v16 and v15 == v16

			fn80 = function()
				local now = os.clock()
				if now - n17 < 3 then
					return
				end

				if _G.BJK_RecLastRun and now - _G.BJK_RecLastRun < 1.5 then
					return
				end

				if now - (_G.BJK_RecWindowStart or 0) > 8 then
					_G.BJK_RecConsecutive = 0
					_G.BJK_RecWindowStart = now
				end

				_G.BJK_RecConsecutive = (_G.BJK_RecConsecutive or 0) + 1
				if _G.BJK_RecConsecutive > 3 then
					return
				end

				if not v15 then
					v15 = fn77()
					if not v15 then
						return
					end
				end

				local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end

				if humanoid2:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or not humanoidRootPart2.Parent then
					return

				n17 = now
				_G.BJK_RecLastRun = now
				local v16 = fn79(v15)

				if v16 and mainConfig.AutoGoToBrainrotOnFailedSteal then
					if _G.invisibleStealEnabled then

							return

							if _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
								_G.toggleInvisibleSteal()
							end
						end)

							RunService_.Heartbeat:Wait()
						end
					end

					pcall(ShowNotification, "FAILED STEAL", "Go To Brainrot")

					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(_G.BJK_goToBrainrot, v15, nil, nil, true)
					end)
				end
			end

			tbl29 = {
				[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
				[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
				[Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
			}

			fn81 = function(old, new)
				if not mainConfig.AutoGoToBrainrotOnFailedSteal then
					return
				end

				if not tbl29[new] then
					return
				end
				n19 = os.clock()

				if os.clock() - n18 <= 1.5 then
					fn80()
				end
			end

			local function fn83()
				local flag7 = localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true

				if flag7 then
					n18 = os.clock()
					pcall(fn78)
				else
					if flag6 and os.clock() - n19 <= 2 and os.clock() - n18 <= 2 then
						fn80()
					elseif os.clock() - n19 <= 2 and os.clock() - n18 <= 2 then
						fn80()
					end

					flag6 = false
				end
			end

			RunService_.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
					n18 = os.clock()
				end
			end)

			pcall(function()
				localPlayer2:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(fn83)
			end)

			fn82 = function(character2)
				local humanoid2 = character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid2 then
					humanoid2.StateChanged:Connect(fn81)
				end
			end

			localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn82)

			if localPlayer2.Character then
				fn82(localPlayer2.Character)
			end
		end

		_G.loadModules = loadModules_
		_G.loadNet = loadNet_
		_G.scanAllPets = scanAllPets_

		_G.setAntiSwapState = function(arg)
			_G.antiSwapEnabled = arg == true
			pcall(ShowNotification, "ANTI-SWAP", _G.antiSwapEnabled and "ATIVADO" or "DESATIVADO")
		end

		_G.toggleAntiSwap = function()
			_G.setAntiSwapState(not _G.antiSwapEnabled)
		end

		handlers[79] = function()
			local character2 = localPlayer2.Character

			if character2 then
				local humanoidRootPart2 = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if humanoidRootPart2 and humanoidRootPart2.Parent then
					return humanoidRootPart2
				end
				local primaryPart = character2.PrimaryPart
				if primaryPart and primaryPart:IsDescendantOf(character2) then
					return primaryPart
				end
			end

			if _G._invisOldRoot and _G._invisOldRoot.Parent then
				return _G._invisOldRoot
			end
			return character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or nil
		end

		_G._getRealHrp = handlers[79]

		do
			local position, v14 = nil, nil
			local tbl29 = {}

			local function fn76(arg)
				arg = arg or 35
				local position2 = handlers[79]() and handlers[79]().Position
				if not position2 then
					return nil
				end
				local v15, v16 = nil, arg

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= localPlayer2 and player_.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart2 = player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoidRootPart2 then
							local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - position2).Magnitude

							if magnitude < v16 then
								v16, v15 = magnitude, player_
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return v15
			end

			local function fn77(arg, arg2)
				arg2 = arg2 or 6

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= localPlayer2 and player_.Character then
						local humanoidRootPart2 = player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - arg).Magnitude <= arg2 then
							return player_
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local n17 = 0

			RunService_.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if _G.TpOnLoadRunning then
					position, v14 = nil, nil
					return
				end

				if not _G.antiSwapEnabled then
					position, v14 = nil, nil
					return
				end

				if not _G.BJK_Ready then
					return
				end
				n17 = n17 + 1
				if n17 % 4 ~= 0 then
					return
				end
				local v15 = fn76(35)
				local v16 = handlers[79]()
				if not v16 then
					return
				end
				local position2 = v16.Position

				if not v15 then
					if v14 and (position2 - v14).Magnitude > 4 then
						local v17 = fn77(v14, 6)

						if v17 then
							local v18 = tbl29[v17.Name]

							if not (v18 and os.clock() - v18 < 8) then
								tbl29[v17.Name] = os.clock()
								local v19 = v17

								task.spawn(function()
									task.wait(0.15)
									local performSwapBack_ = _G.performSwapBack or performSwapBack

									if not performSwapBack_ then
										task.wait(1)
										performSwapBack_ = _G.performSwapBack or performSwapBack
									end

									if performSwapBack_ then
										pcall(performSwapBack_, v19)
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end

					position, v14 = nil, position2
					return
				end

				local v17 = tbl29[v15.Name]
				if v17 and os.clock() - v17 < 8 then
					position, v14 = v15.Character and v15.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v15.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position or position, position2
					return
				end
				local humanoidRootPart2 = v15.Character and v15.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart2 then
					position, v14 = nil, position2
					return
				end
				local position3 = humanoidRootPart2.Position

				if position and v14 then
					local magnitude = (position3 - position).Magnitude
					local flag6 = (position3 - v14).Magnitude < 6
					local flag7 = (position2 - v14).Magnitude > 2.5

					if magnitude > 2.5 and flag6 and flag7 then
						tbl29[v15.Name] = os.clock()
						local v18 = v15

						task.spawn(function()
							task.wait(0.15)

							if _G.invisibleStealEnabled and _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
								pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
								task.wait(0.08)
							end

							local performSwapBack_ = _G.performSwapBack or performSwapBack

							if not performSwapBack_ then
								task.wait(1)
								performSwapBack_ = _G.performSwapBack or performSwapBack
							end

							if performSwapBack_ then
								pcall(performSwapBack_, v18)
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				position, v14 = position3, position2
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local n17, n18, position, tbl29, flag6, fn76
			n17 = 0
			n18 = 2.5
			position = nil
			tbl29 = {}
			flag6 = false

			fn76 = function(arg, arg2)
				arg2 = arg2 or 7

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
						local character2 = player_.Character

						if character2 then
							local humanoidRootPart2 = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							if humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart2.Position - arg).Magnitude <= arg2 then
								return player_
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function performSwapBack_(arg)
				if not arg then
					return false
				end

				if os.clock() - n17 < n18 then
					return false
				end
				n17 = os.clock()

				for i = 1, 10 do
					char = player.Character
					hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if char and char.Parent == Workspace and hum and hum.Parent and hum.Health > 0 then
						break
					end
					task.wait(0.05)
				end

				if not hum or not char or char.Parent ~= Workspace then
					return false
				end
				local bodySwapPotion = player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Body Swap Potion") or char and char:FindFirstChild("Body Swap Potion")

				if not bodySwapPotion then
					for i, child in ipairs(player.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name:lower():find("swap") then
							bodySwapPotion = child
							break
						end
					end

					if not bodySwapPotion and char then
						for i, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name:lower():find("swap") then
								bodySwapPotion = child
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if not bodySwapPotion then
					return false
				end

				for i = 1, 2 do
					pcall(function()
						hum:EquipTool(bodySwapPotion)
					end)

					task.wait(0.08)
					if bodySwapPotion.Parent == char then
						break
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.05)
				local remoteEvent = bodySwapPotion:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent") or bodySwapPotion:FindFirstChild("RemoteClick") or bodySwapPotion:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
				local flag7 = false

				if remoteEvent then
					for i = 1, 2 do
						local ok = pcall(function()
							remoteEvent:FireServer(arg)
						end)

						if ok then
							flag7 = true
							break
						end
						task.wait(0.08)
					end

					if not flag7 then
						pcall(function()
							bodySwapPotion:Activate()
						end)
					else
						task.wait(0.05)

						pcall(function()
							bodySwapPotion:Activate()
						end)
					end
				else
					for i = 1, 2 do
						pcall(function()
							bodySwapPotion:Activate()
						end)

						task.wait(0.08)
						if bodySwapPotion.Parent ~= char and bodySwapPotion.Parent ~= player.Backpack then
							break
						end
					end
				end

				task.wait(0.08)
				return true
			end

			_G.performSwapBack = performSwapBack_

			localPlayer2:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
				local flag7 = localPlayer2:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true

				if flag6 and not flag7 and _G.antiSwapEnabled then
					if position then
						local v14 = handlers[79]()

						if v14 then
							local magnitude = (v14.Position - position).Magnitude

							if magnitude > 12 then
								local v15 = fn76(position, 7)

								if v15 then
									task.wait(0.03)
									local v16 = performSwapBack_(v15)

									if v16 then
										position = v14.Position

										for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
											if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
												local character2 = player_.Character
												local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

												if humanoidRootPart2 then
													tbl29[player_.UserId] = humanoidRootPart2.Position
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				flag6 = flag7
			end)

			while true do
				if _G.BJK_HubGen ~= bjkHubGen then
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
				if _G.TpOnLoadRunning then
					continue
				end

				if not _G.antiSwapEnabled then
					continue
				end
				local v14 = handlers[79]()
				if not v14 then
					position = nil
					continue
				end
				local position2 = v14.Position

				if not position then
					position = position2

					for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
						if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
							local character2 = player_.Character
							local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if humanoidRootPart2 then
								tbl29[player_.UserId] = humanoidRootPart2.Position
							end
						end
					end

					continue
				end

				local magnitude = (position2 - position).Magnitude

				if magnitude > 15 then
					local v15 = fn76(position, 7)

					if v15 then
						local v16 = tbl29[v15.UserId]

						if not v16 or (v16 - position2).Magnitude > 4 then
							task.wait(0.03)
							local v17 = performSwapBack_(v15)

							if v17 then
								position = position2

								for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
									if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
										local character2 = player_.Character
										local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

										if humanoidRootPart2 then
											tbl29[player_.UserId] = humanoidRootPart2.Position
										end
									end
								end
							end

							task.wait(1)
							continue
						end
					end
				end

				position = position2

				for i, player_ in ipairs(Players3:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= localPlayer2 then
						local character2 = player_.Character
						local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoidRootPart2 then
							tbl29[player_.UserId] = humanoidRootPart2.Position
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end)()
end

task.spawn(function()
	pcall(_G.loadModules)
	pcall(_G.loadNet)
end)

task.spawn(function()
	if _G._bjkScanWarmed and _G.BJKScannerReady then
		return
	end
	pcall(_G.loadModules)
	pcall(_G.loadNet)
	local now = os.clock()
	local n, n2 = -1, 0
	local n3 = _G.BJK_IS_MOBILE and 0.35 or 0.1
	local n4 = _G.BJK_IS_MOBILE and 8 or 12

	repeat
		local ok, result, result2 = pcall(_G.scanAllPets)

		if ok and result then
			local n5 = #result

			if n5 == n then
				n2 = n2 + 1
			else
				n2 = 0
				n = n5
			end

			if n5 > 0 and ((result2 or 0) == 0 and n2 >= 1 or n2 >= 6) then
				break
			end
		end

		task.wait(n3)
	until os.clock() - now > n4

	_G._BJKStarted = true
	_G.BJKScannerReady = true
end)

if not _G.MainConfig.TpSettings.TpOnLoad and not _G._uiBuilt then
	local ok, result = pcall(_G._buildUI)
end


task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) do
		pcall(function()
			if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
				if localPlayer2.CameraMaxZoomDistance ~= 128 then
					localPlayer2.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 128
				end

				if localPlayer2.CameraMinZoomDistance > 0.5 then
					localPlayer2.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
				end

				if localPlayer2.DevCameraOcclusionMode ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
					localPlayer2.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
